The `public contributions` by users in Saudi Arabia on `2026/10/23 3:12 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Saudi Arabia` and cities `Riyadh` `Jeddah` `Mecca` `Medina` `Khamismushait` `Dammam` `Abha`.

There are `138 countries` and `674 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users).

There are `989 users`  in Saudi Arabia. You need at least `11 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Saudi Arabia GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/saudi_arabia.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
